### SECTION 2 (affected by repeal, see note) — Provisions related to definitions in Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 4 (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note). Framework for criteria on maintaining the agricultural area in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) For the purposes of the point (ii) of Article 4(1)(c) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the criteria that farmers are to meet in order to fulfil the obligation to maintain the agricultural area in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation without preparatory action going beyond usual agricultural methods and machineries shall be established by *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-b4fa34e5be20d359c0fc83c385841e11] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] in either or both of the following ways: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- ((a)) the relevant authority requires[^c-6a435f58892b08029bdf239e56e9f542] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] at least one annual activity to be carried out by a farmer. Where justified for environmental reasons, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-abb2a0f65b9a968a250b25e7c5248ff9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may decide to recognise also activities that are carried out only every second year; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((b)) the relevant authority sets[^c-8c57537a8f307d3b0507cbacac05232a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] out the characteristics to be met by an agricultural area in order to be deemed maintained in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) When establishing criteria referred to in paragraph 1, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-f333b336cc674a55a8a915c909075352] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may distinguish between different types of agricultural areas. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-f892c7380064f9ba4fb5a3bfa35ce306] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 5 (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note). Framework for minimum activities on agricultural areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-41e67a7fc918645f59d8aa7a3cb133d1][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For the purposes of the point (iii) of Article 4(1)(c) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the minimum activity to be established by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-41e67a7fc918645f59d8aa7a3cb133d1] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority[^c-e48660a2bf877ecf6cec6a51016ce971] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-41e67a7fc918645f59d8aa7a3cb133d1] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] that is to be carried out on agricultural areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation shall be at least one annual activity to be carried out by a farmer. Where justified for environmental reasons, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-41e67a7fc918645f59d8aa7a3cb133d1] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-06f4f94ae383b94ca6a2239eb34a1afa] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-41e67a7fc918645f59d8aa7a3cb133d1] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may decide to recognise also activities that are carried out only every second year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-41e67a7fc918645f59d8aa7a3cb133d1] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 6 (affected by repeal, see note). Predominance of grasses and other herbaceous forage in case of permanent grassland (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For the purposes of Article 4(1)(h) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, grasses and other herbaceous forage shall be deemed to remain predominant where they cover more than 50 % of the eligible area at the level of the agricultural parcel within the meaning of Article 67(4)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 7 (affected by repeal, see note). Established local practices in case of permanent grassland (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-aee83d5c5ffc643ea8fdaf28f343de13][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For the purposes of Article 4(1)(h) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, established local practices shall be practices for areas for livestock grazing which are traditional in character and are commonly applied on the areas concerned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 8 (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note). Reduction coefficient according to Article 32(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-c18ef2707d819e5db35f3ef3a8ed1149][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

When applying Article 32(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 for permanent grassland which can be grazed and which forms part of established local practices where grasses and other herbaceous forage are traditionally not predominant in grazing areas, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c18ef2707d819e5db35f3ef3a8ed1149] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-0db3221120d9144a8e58c463afd11bc3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c18ef2707d819e5db35f3ef3a8ed1149] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may distinguish between different categories of areas in order to apply different reduction coefficients to such categories *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c18ef2707d819e5db35f3ef3a8ed1149] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] , provided that such a decision was made and notified to the Commission no later than 31 January 2015[^c-fd112d89388ed91b5df78ea913c53859] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-c18ef2707d819e5db35f3ef3a8ed1149] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15].

#### Article 9 (affected by repeal, see note). Hemp (affected by repeal, see note)[^c000001][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.)[^c000002] For the purposes of Article 32(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the eligibility of areas used for the production of hemp shall be subject to the use of seed of the varieties listed in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] Annex IV[^c-88cba469a06758dc63856193fae60e48] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15].

(2. (Extent: NI) (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] The relevant authority[^c-723ab4a7e8a5d0bac2f44fda69f67482] *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall establish the system for determining the Δ9-tetrahydrocannabinol content (hereinafter referred to as ‘THC content’) in hemp varieties, which allows them to apply the method set out in Annex III. *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3. (Extent: NI) (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] The *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority[^c-1f16a63c50914162e5ba7817a053d48a] *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall keep the records related to findings on the THC content. Such records shall comprise for each variety at least the results in terms of THC content from each sample expressed in percentage to two decimal places, the procedure used, the number of tests carried out, an indication of the point at which the sample was taken and measures taken at national level. *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(4. (Extent: NI) (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] If an average of all the samples of a given variety exceeds the THC content as laid down in Article 32(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-4f9334f2088d3753247aa911968b45e3] *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall use procedure B as described in Annex III to this Regulation for the variety concerned in the course of the following claim year. That procedure shall be used in the course of the next claim years unless all the analytical results for the given variety are below the THC content as laid down in Article 32(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-4a3e761c6e0ae56a3f8a2403f2cbe811] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(5.)[^c-25722f4cd7721307ec72f1d47ac1df35] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(6.) For the purposes of this Regulation, ‘hemp cultivated as catch crop’ means crop of hemp sown after 30 June of a given year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(7.)[^c-8b10879f4a637e371b7dd9895a2ae339] Crops of hemp shall continue to be cultivated under normal growing conditions in accordance with local practice for at least 10 days from the date of the end of flowering so that the checks necessary for the application of this Article can be made. Hemp cultivated as catch crop shall continue to be cultivated under normal growing conditions in accordance with local practice at least until the end of the vegetation period. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8b10879f4a637e371b7dd9895a2ae339] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

However, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8b10879f4a637e371b7dd9895a2ae339] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-45034b748f1c7f40043737f45555c3ab] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8b10879f4a637e371b7dd9895a2ae339] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may authorise hemp to be harvested after flowering has begun but before the end of the 10-day period after the end of flowering, provided that the inspectors indicate which representative parts of each plot concerned must continue to be cultivated for at least 10 days following the end of flowering for inspection purposes, in accordance with the method set out in Annex III. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-8b10879f4a637e371b7dd9895a2ae339] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

### SECTION 3 (affected by repeal, see note) — Active farmer (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 10 (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note). Cases where agricultural areas are mainly areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_F_1d203e2f-a541-4fef-f064-fc3812950bc8][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) For the purposes of Article 9(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, a natural or legal person, or a group of natural or legal persons, shall be considered as having agricultural areas which are mainly areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation, where such areas represent more than 50 % of all agricultural area declared in accordance with Article 72(1)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_1d203e2f-a541-4fef-f064-fc3812950bc8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) Article 9(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 shall not apply to a natural or legal person, or a group of natural or legal persons who carry out, on areas naturally kept in a state suitable for grazing or cultivation, an agricultural activity within the meaning of the point (i) of Article 4(1)(c) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^M_F_1d203e2f-a541-4fef-f064-fc3812950bc8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 11 (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note). Receipts obtained from non-agricultural activities (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) For the purposes of point (a) of the third subparagraph of Article 9(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and, where appropriate, Article 13 of this Regulation, receipts obtained from agricultural activities shall be those receipts that have been received by a farmer from his agricultural activity within the meaning of Article 4(1)(c) of that Regulation on his holding, including the Union support under the European Agricultural Guarantee Fund (EAGF) and the European Agricultural Fund for Rural Development (EAFRD), as well as any national aid granted for agricultural activities *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-f2a722338e9b2cbc7c3ea3eb109164f1]....

Receipts from processing of agricultural products within the meaning of Article 4(1)(d) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 of the holding shall be deemed as receipts from agricultural activities provided that the products processed remain the ownership of the farmer and that such processing results in another agricultural product within the meaning of Article 4(1)(d) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Any other receipts shall be considered to be receipts from non-agricultural activities. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) For the purposes of paragraph 1, ‘receipts’ means gross receipts before deduction of related costs and taxes. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3.)[^c-6a1e0c529cd2240dfa099a2178e995e6] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 12 (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note). Amount of direct payments referred to in Article 9(2) and (4) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and in Article 13(2) of this Regulation (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) The annual amount of direct payments of a farmer referred to in point (a) of the third subparagraph of Article 9(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and, where appropriate, in Article 13(2) of this Regulation, shall be the total amount of direct payments to which the farmer was entitled in accordance with Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 for the most recent fiscal year for which evidence on receipts from non-agricultural activities is available. That amount shall be calculated without taking into account the application of Articles 63 and 91(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where the most recent fiscal year referred to in the first subparagraph is 2014 or earlier, the annual amount of direct payments shall be the total amount of direct payments to which the farmer was entitled in accordance with Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 before the reductions and exclusions provided for in Articles 21 and 23 of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) Where a farmer did not submit an aid application for direct payments in accordance with Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 in the most recent fiscal year referred to in the first subparagraph of paragraph 1, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-994838e11515943829d0ced749a13bda] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall establish the total amount of direct payments referred to in the first subparagraph of paragraph 1 by multiplying the number of eligible hectares declared by that farmer in the year of submitting the aid application in accordance with Article 72(1)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 by the national average direct support payment per hectare for the year referred to in the first subparagraph of paragraph 1. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The national average direct support payment per hectare referred to in the first subparagraph shall be established by dividing the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority’s share of the national ceiling[^c-ab16d65f171084f72b82f4ab00d4b7b2] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] for that year by the total number of eligible hectares declared in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the constituent nation[^c-0336e4fb3646765f81dd5d034f96bd24] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] for that year in accordance with Article 72(1)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where the year referred to in the first subparagraph of paragraph 1 is 2014 or earlier, the national average direct support payment per hectare referred to in the first subparagraph of this paragraph shall be established by dividing the national ceiling set out in Annex VIII to Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 of that year by the total number of eligible hectares declared in that Member State for that year in accordance with Article 19(1)(a) of Regulation (EC) No 73/2009. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3.) The amount of direct payments of a farmer referred to in Article 9(4) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 shall be the total amount of direct payments to which the farmer was entitled in accordance with Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 before the application of Articles 63 and 91(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 for the previous year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where the year referred to in the first subparagraph is 2014, the amount of direct payments shall be the total amount of direct payments for the year 2014 to which the farmer was entitled pursuant to Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 before the reductions and exclusions provided for in Articles 21 and 23 of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(4.) Where a farmer did not submit an aid application for direct payments in accordance with Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 for the previous year as referred to in the first subparagraph of paragraph 3, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-e3368460849716694ccd8bdd96b5f9a2] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall establish the total amount of direct payments referred to in the first subparagraph of paragraph 3 by multiplying the number of eligible hectares declared by that farmer in the year of submitting the aid application in accordance with Article 72(1)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 by the national average direct support payment per hectare for the previous year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The national average direct support payment per hectare referred to in the first subparagraph shall be established by dividing the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority’s share of the national ceiling[^c-ecb8f2b6b3fb3cda4f3044c51f0122d6] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] for that year by the total number of eligible hectares declared in *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the constituent nation[^c-6a1a322000812d7bb1c77605b86cfd52] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] for that year in accordance with Article 72(1)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where the previous year referred to in first subparagraph of paragraph 3 is 2014, Member States shall establish the annual amount of direct payments of that farmer by multiplying the number of eligible hectares declared by that farmer for the year 2015 in accordance with Article 72(1)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 by the national average direct support payment per hectare for the year 2014. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The national average direct support payment per hectare for the year 2014 shall be established by dividing the national ceiling for the year 2014 set out in Annex VIII to Regulation (EC) No 73/2009 by the total number of eligible hectares declared in that Member State for the year 2014 in accordance with Article 19(1)(a) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(5.)[^c-59f31b2e25bcfdb19b9f69e12704c80f] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 13 (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note). Criteria for proving that agricultural activities are not insignificant and that the principal business or company objects consist of exercising an agricultural activity (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) For the purposes of point (b) of the third subparagraph of Article 9(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, agricultural activities are not insignificant if the total receipts obtained from agricultural activities within the meaning of Article 11 of this Regulation in the most recent fiscal year for which such evidence is available represent at least one third of the total receipts obtained in the most recent fiscal year for which such evidence is available. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

[^c-8746bac458460137638b370774bc3d4c]...

By way of derogation from the first *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] subparagraph[^c-401506258c61ff30a1f42da6ee85801a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15], the relevant authority may use[^c-a3598fe9bcb8b6af2e26804d6c68da55] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] alternative criteria allowing an entity to demonstrate that its agricultural activities are not insignificant pursuant to point (b) of the third subparagraph of Article 9(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] , provided those criteria were established by the relevant authority prior to exit day[^c-49f03437d1beecb12b0b5a199a240460] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15].

(2.) For the purposes of point (a) of Article 9(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-96f6fe4bae087920db66c1783d6339a6] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may decide that agricultural activities form only an insignificant part of the overall economic activities of a natural or legal person, or a group of natural or legal persons, by using the following *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] method[^c-86bddb227bfa1fef161bce6c15f8657e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]:

- ((a)) the annual amount of direct payments is less than 5 % of the total receipts obtained from non-agricultural activities within the meaning of Article 11 of this Regulation in the most recent fiscal year for which such evidence is available; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((b)) [^c-554a1885386d0f262fdc5b4db980c1f8]...

[^c-5168961495fd80d511681814b716b15f]...

(3.) For the purposes of point (c) of the third subparagraph of Article 9(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and, where appropriate, point (b) of Article 9(3) of that Regulation, an agricultural activity shall be considered to be the principal business or company object of a legal person if recorded as a principal business or company object in the official business register or any equivalent official evidence of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-7d708ce2184af4c935496ab9be84fa80] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]. In the case of a natural person, equivalent evidence shall be required. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where no such registers exist, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-4c74243cd62f468c3d83687357ca3f4e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall use equivalent evidence. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

By way of derogation from the first and second subparagraphs, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority may, applying such criteria as the authority established prior to exit day, determine whether[^c-910ca0e6babf3b3834400125be787b2e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] an agricultural activity is to be considered to be a principal business or company object of a natural or legal person pursuant point (c) of the third subparagraph of Article 9(2) and, where appropriate, to point (b) of Article 9(3) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-fdd578db28f855fa2ed11ab896a0be15] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 23 (affected by repeal, see note). Calculation of the value of payment entitlements (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) Payment entitlements shall in a first step be calculated up to three decimals and in a second step be rounded up or down to the nearest second decimal. If the calculation gives a result where the third decimal is a 5, the sum shall be rounded up to the second decimal. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) If a farmer transfers a fraction of an entitlement, the value of that fraction shall be calculated proportionally for each remaining relevant year as referred to in Article 25 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-5cbb8a270c901744829607e4217ccc57]... of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3.) The relevant authority[^c-9e3a283b869947e7e03b865525107e96] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may modify payment entitlements by merging fractions of entitlements owned by a farmer. The value of the merged entitlements shall be determined for each remaining relevant year as referred to in Article 25 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-f4e7c9079cea3832e99865af7f134666]... of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 by adding the value of the fractions. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 24 (affected by repeal, see note). Requirements for activation of payment entitlements (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) Payment entitlements may only be declared for payment once per year by the farmer who holds them (owned or leased-in) at the latest date for lodging the single application. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

However, where a farmer uses the possibility to amend the single application in accordance with the rules established *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-e8a732ae3f3a0209ece250d73e3c3f3c]... on the basis of Article 78(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, he may also declare for payment those payment entitlements which he holds (owned or leased-in) at the date of his notification of the amendments to the competent authority, provided that the payment entitlements concerned are not declared for payment by another farmer in respect of the same year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where the farmer acquires payment entitlements by way of a transfer from another farmer and where that other farmer had already declared those payment entitlements for payment, the additional declaration of those payment entitlements by the transferee shall only be admissible if the transferor has already informed the competent authority of the transfer in accordance with the rules established *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-5986422be4f1ea99c155b6f05adf8cf2]... on the basis of Article 34(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and withdraws those payment entitlements from his own single application, within the time-limits for amending the single application fixed *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-5986422be4f1ea99c155b6f05adf8cf2]... on the basis of Article 78 (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.)[^c-a7a5324c5af9f924a462f693c4d5485e] Where a farmer declares a number of payment entitlements exceeding his total eligible area declared pursuant to Article 33(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the payment entitlement or the fraction of a payment entitlement which is partially exceeding that eligible area shall be deemed as fully activated for the purposes of Article 31(1)(b) of that Regulation. However, the payment shall be calculated on the basis of the corresponding fraction of an eligible hectare. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-a7a5324c5af9f924a462f693c4d5485e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) Payment entitlements may be transferred at any time of the year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) Where *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-069a096f692896be41e97fc89a2f79d3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] uses the option provided for in Article 34(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, it shall define the regions referred to in that provision in the first year of application of Article 34(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and at the latest one month before the date fixed by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority[^c-3b1770f324f68f9095ddac53d36d02ce] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] pursuant to Article 33(1) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Subsection 3 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

**National or regional reserves *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]**

#### Article 26 (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note). Reversion to the national or regional reserve due to retention on transfer of payment entitlements (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-3c494677a986c78e8bdb848886711c05] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] uses the option provided for in Article 34(4) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, it may decide, in accordance with objective criteria and in such a way as to ensure equal treatment between farmers and to avoid market and competition distortion, to revert to the national or regional reserve up to 30 % of the annual unit values of each payment entitlement transferred without the corresponding eligible hectares within the meaning of Article 32(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, or the equivalent amount expressed in number of payment entitlements. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

By way of derogation from the first subparagraph, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-4b57cc7f0e237f5a4b8f3f47d7189816] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may provide for a reversion up to 50 % of the annual unit value of each payment entitlement or the equivalent amount expressed in number of payment entitlements as referred to in the first subparagraph during the first three years of application of the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] basic payment scheme *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ed56b4a29b0d4bd8e4b6328c1c9eba81] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]farm sustainability transition payment scheme *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15].

In this subsection references to “national or regional reserve” are to be construed in accordance with the definitions contained in Article 4 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013.[^c-a9f5735c359b50f90aa36276bf8db9e4] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-72e11c30f3b8ff41ec4dd2033cdab275] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 27 (affected by repeal, see note). Application of the windfall profit clause (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-734fc0196067557df06e9c43c0f29543][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 28 (affected by repeal, see note). Establishment of payment entitlements from the national or regional reserve under Article 30(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) For the purposes of Article 30(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, where a young farmer or a farmer who commences his agricultural activity applies for payment entitlements from the national or regional reserve while he does not hold any payment entitlement (owned or leased-in), he shall receive a number of payment entitlements equal to the number of eligible hectares he holds (owned or leased-in) at the latest date for lodging his application for the allocation or increase of the value of payment entitlements fixed *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-9a2d3489d0911c88ef7c2c1ad93ae87e]... on the basis of Article 78(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) Where a young farmer or a farmer who commences his agricultural activity applies for payment entitlements from the national or regional reserve while he already holds payment entitlements (owned or leased-in), he shall receive a number of payment entitlements equal to the number of eligible hectares he holds (owned or leased-in) at the latest date for lodging his application referred to in paragraph 1 for which he does not hold any payment entitlement (owned or leased-in). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where the value of the entitlements that the farmer already holds (owned or leased-in) is below the national or regional average referred to in the second subparagraph of Article 30(8) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the annual unit values of those entitlements may be increased up to the national or regional average as provided for in Article 30(10) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

However, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] if the relevant authority applies[^c-834890a3d487dfeac0695be64efd8a6e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the increase referred to in Article 30(10) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 for the purposes of Article 30(7) of that Regulation, the increase referred to in the second subparagraph of this paragraph shall be mandatory *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-2fc7fb29cb37bd0b60c5787d35a7c1aa].... Such increase should be at a level corresponding to the highest level of increase applied for the purposes of Article 30(7) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3.) If the relevant authority applied[^c-1706e5a26e6e74f775ee5e934d41fb04] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] Article 24(6) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-274ac7b05c60ddbdac07bba208dba93f]... of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] as it had effect immediately before exit day[^c-d72558be91a84b86322e491a8b23acae] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15], the limitations of the allocation of payment entitlements laid down in those provisions may be applied *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] *mutatis mutandis *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]* for the allocation of payment entitlements pursuant to Article 30(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

However, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-e24e3997f0a6b4fd7ff491a03ebb6806] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may decide that where the application of one or several limitations referred to in the first subparagraph limits the total number of payment entitlements which the farmer already holds and which are to be newly allocated from the reserve to less than a fixed percentage of his eligible hectares in the year in which he applies for allocation of payment entitlements from the reserve, that farmer shall be allocated an additional number of payment entitlements corresponding to a share in the total number of his eligible hectares declared in his application for that year in accordance with Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The fixed percentage referred to in the second subparagraph of this Article shall be calculated in accordance with the method referred in the second subparagraph of Article 31(2) of this Regulation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] as it had effect immediately before exit day[^c-fe0570234abb22ee858678db275960eb] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15].

The share of the total number of eligible hectares of the farmer referred to in the second subparagraph of this paragraph shall be calculated as half of the difference in percentage points between the fixed percentage referred to in the third subparagraph of this paragraph and the share of ‘payment entitlements held by the farmer’ in his eligible hectares declared in accordance with Article 72(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 in his application for the year referred to in the second subparagraph of this paragraph. For the purposes of this subparagraph, ‘payment entitlements held by the farmer’ means payments entitlement already held by the farmer and which are to be newly allocated from the reserve. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

When calculating the number of eligible hectares referred to in the second, third and fourth subparagraphs of this paragraph, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may decide not to include any areas taken up by permanent crops, by permanent grassland located in areas with difficult climatic conditions as referred to in Article 24(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] as it had effect immediately before exit day[^c-4af7ccc34dadcb8fa465f48ffcb37576] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] or by areas recognised as permanent grassland in accordance with the second subparagraph of Article 4(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The relevant authority[^c-19186d4378d029128bfdaf9be505f7e0] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] using the possibility provided in Article 23 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 to apply the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] basic payment scheme *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-cac56d867253f3d09a5ca39b8d740c82] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]farm sustainability transition payment scheme *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] at regional level may base the calculation method referred to in the second subparagraph of this paragraph upon the total numbers allocated/declared in 2015 in the relevant region. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For the purpose of determining the threshold in the second subparagraph, the land acquired or leased-in by the farmer after 19 October 2011 shall not be taken into account. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(4.) For the purposes of this Article, only those farmers who commence their agricultural activity shall be considered who commenced their agricultural activity in calendar year 2013 or any later year and who submit an application for the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] basic payment *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-bf83382a3e85c2542b831fcea93c4800] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]farm sustainability transition payment scheme *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] not later than two years after the calendar year in which they commenced their agricultural activity. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 29 (affected by repeal, see note). Establishment of payment entitlements from the national or regional reserve under Article 30(7) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) For the purposes of Article 30(7) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, where new payment entitlements are allocated as provided for in Article 30(10) of that Regulation, they shall be allocated in accordance with the conditions laid down in this Article and in accordance with the objective criteria laid down by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority[^c-616ed33ecb5c7abf6c7f033f472c9ab3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] concerned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) Where a farmer who does not hold any payment entitlement (owned or leased-in) is entitled in accordance with Article 30(7) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 to receive payment entitlements from the national or regional reserve and applies for it, he shall receive a number of payment entitlements up to the number of eligible hectares he holds (owned or leased-in) at the latest date for lodging his application as referred to in Article 28(1). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3.) Where a farmer who holds payment entitlements (owned or leased-in) is entitled in accordance with Article 30(7) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 to receive payment entitlements from the national or regional reserve and applies for it, he shall receive a number of payment entitlements up to the number of eligible hectares he holds (owned or leased-in) at the latest date for lodging his application as referred to in Article 28(1) for which he does not hold any payment entitlement (owned or leased-in). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- ((a)) covering organic soils with a high percentage of organic carbon, such as peat land or wetlands; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((b)) hosting habitats listed in Annex I to Directive 92/43/EEC or protected under national legislation; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((c)) hosting plant species listed in Annex II to Directive 92/43/EEC or protected under national legislation; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((d)) being of significant importance for wild bird species listed in Annex I to Directive 2009/147/EC; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((e)) being of significant importance for wild animal species protected under Directive 92/43/EEC or protected under national legislation; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((f)) [^M_F_414d252b-f632-41d7-e384-ab842b9952c7]...
- ((g)) covering soils with a high risk of erosion; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((h)) being located in a sensitive area designated within the river basin management plans pursuant to Directive 2000/60/EC. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 42 (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note). Reconversion in case of non-respect of the obligation on environmentally sensitive permanent grassland areas (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Without prejudice to Directive 2004/35/CE of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^f00024], where a farmer has converted or ploughed permanent grassland that is subject to the obligation referred to in the third subparagraph of Article 45(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority[^c-fa538aa4e176094eb65edd8e8d04c528] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] concerned shall provide for the obligation to reconvert the area into permanent grassland and may, on a case by case basis, issue precise instructions to be respected by the farmer concerned on how to reverse the environmental damage caused in order to restore the environmentally sensitive status. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The farmer shall be informed without delay after the non-compliance has been established of the obligation to reconvert and of the date before which that obligation is to be complied with. By way of derogation from Article 4(1)(h) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, the land reconverted shall be considered as permanent grassland as of the first day of reconversion and be subject to the obligation referred to in the third subparagraph of Article 45(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Such areas shall not include areas under crops normally sown for harvesting. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] The relevant authority may[^c-9752ba76dedca3fecc902bc2361413f5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] establish further requirements. Such areas may include herbaceous species, provided that melliferous crops remain predominant. Without prejudice to the ‘no production’ requirement established in paragraph 10a, hives may be put on areas of land lying fallow for melliferous plants (pollen- and nectar-rich species). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

By way of derogation from Article 4(1)(h) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, land lying fallow and land lying fallow for melliferous plants (pollen- and nectar-rich species) for the purpose of fulfilling the ecological focus area for more than five years shall remain arable land. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3.)[^c-17c92696871c92fb2eb25303170c25a5] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(4.) Landscape features shall be at the disposal of the farmer and may be those that are protected under GAEC 7, SMR 2 or SMR 3 as referred to in Annex II to Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and/or one or more of the following features: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- ((a)) hedges, wooded strips or trees in line; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((b)) isolated trees; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((c)) field copses including trees, bushes or stones; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((d)) ponds. Reservoirs made of concrete or plastic shall not be considered ecological focus areas; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((e)) ditches, including open watercourses for the purpose of irrigation or drainage. Channels with walls of concrete shall not be considered ecological focus areas. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((f)) traditional stone walls. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where the legislation applying in the constituent nation so provides,[^c-b7d04deb0c2f28e3661d84bf6768a32f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the selection of landscape features *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall be limited[^c-ba0cd414e91cb2d10168cca363084c8b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] to those under GAEC 7, SMR 2 or SMR 3 as referred to in Annex II to Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 and/or to one or more of points (a) to (f) of the first subparagraph. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For the hedges, wooded strips and trees in line as well as ditches referred to in points (a) and (e) of the first subparagraph, respectively, the area to be qualified as ecological focus area shall be calculated up to a maximum width of 10 metres. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For the field copses and ponds referred to in points (c) and (d) of the first subparagraph, respectively, the area to be qualified as ecological focus area shall be calculated up to a maximum size of 0,3 hectare. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For the purposes of point (d) of the first subparagraph, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the[^c-2fd28035c3fa07633315400492d2755e] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] minimum size for ponds *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] is as set by the relevant authority prior to exit day[^c-19a8e68824b2dcbbc649a00d171185a2] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]. Where there is a strip with riparian vegetation along the water the corresponding area shall be included for the purpose of calculating the ecological focus area. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] The relevant authority[^c-fa56cf7eb9f80618e2a8280949c95227] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may establish criteria to ensure that ponds are of natural value, taking into account the role that natural ponds play for the conservation of habitats and species. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For the purposes of point (f) of the first subparagraph, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the[^c-55017ec1fdef04b85bf2de9a90a885d1] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] minimum criteria based on national or regional specificities, including limits to the dimensions of height and width *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] is as established by the relevant authority prior to exit day[^c-749e0d5403e9c1e1c369ed417faf43e1] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15].

(5.) Buffer strips and field margins may be any buffer strips and field margins including those buffer strips along water courses required under GAEC 1, SMR 1 or SMR 10 as referred to in Annex II to Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 or field margins protected under GAEC 7, SMR 2 or SMR 3 as referred to in that Annex. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The relevant authority[^c-17ed183e5d2a3e3ed6920a48e67e7b24] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall not limit the selection of buffer strips and field margins to those required under the cross compliance rules referred to in the first subparagraph. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Nothing in this Article shall prevent the relevant authority from establishing, using any power the relevant authority has, the minimum width of buffer strips and field margins.[^c-42015bdbd5ea117de6db7510a51549e3] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] Along water courses, riparian vegetation shall be included for the purpose of calculating the ecological focus area. There shall be no agricultural production on buffer strips and field margins. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For buffer strips and field margins other than those required or protected under GAEC 1, GAEC 7, SMR 1, SMR 2, SMR 3 or SMR 10 as referred to in Annex II to Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013, the area to be qualified as ecological focus area shall be calculated up to a maximum width of 20 metres. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(6.) Hectares of agro-forestry shall be arable land eligible for the basic payment scheme *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-2ac5915eeea498262e9ea6511e2a5f6d]... referred to in Chapter 1 of Title III of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and fulfilling the conditions for which support under Article 44 of Regulation (EC) No 1698/2005 or Article 23 of Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013 was or is granted. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(8.) The species that may be used for areas with short rotation coppice with no use of mineral fertiliser or plant protection products or both, are those species which have been selected by the relevant authority prior to exit day, from the list established pursuant to Article 4(2)(c) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, as being the most suitable from an ecological perspective.[^c-464b9677fd7453fa0bcb36f92b790864] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]The relevant authority must also apply any requirements established by them prior to exit day[^c-59ce8b5ead5a7fb34fc57edf7c518272] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] as regards the use of mineral fertilisers and/or plant protection products in case *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority authorises[^c-5b0d1c11747ce0e24f78d66594f00f91] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] their use, keeping in mind the objective of ecological focus areas in particular to safeguard and improve biodiversity. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(8a.)[^c000005] On areas with *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] *Miscanthus *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]* and *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] *Silphium perfoliatum *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]* the relevant authority[^c-845f8afcc522db24f164a54e3c94ce02] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall ban the use of plant protection products except in the first year in which the two species are established by a farmer. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] The relevant authority[^c-38ad9f554c012793885fa8d42f3f04e8] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall ban or establish requirements as regards the use of mineral fertilisers on these areas, taking into account the objective of the ecological focus areas in particular to safeguard and improve biodiversity. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(9.) Areas under catch crops or green cover shall include such areas established pursuant to the requirements under SMR 1 as referred to in Annex II to Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 as well as other areas under catch crops or green cover, on the condition that they were established by sowing a mixture of crop species or by under-sowing grass or leguminous crops in the main crop. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

This period shall not be less than 8 weeks. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] Nothing in this Article prevents the relevant authority from establishing, using any power the relevant authority has,[^c-32417a90d4c68d33faa8948d5ee83bd4] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] additional conditions notably with regard to production methods. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Areas under catch crops or green cover shall not include areas under winter crops which are sown in autumn normally for harvesting or for grazing. They shall also not include the areas covered with equivalent practices mentioned in points I.3 and 4 of Annex IX to Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(10.) On areas with nitrogen-fixing crops, farmers shall grow those nitrogen-fixing crops which are included in a list established by the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority prior to exit day[^c-5731b29f6821a75644b9d59ace9dea4a] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]. That list shall contain the nitrogen-fixing crops that the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority[^c-80451ff332f4837d5bc36a19914411c9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] considers as contributing to the objective of improving biodiversity and may include mixtures of nitrogen-fixing crops with other crops provided that nitrogen-fixing crop species are predominant. Those crops shall be present during the growing season. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] Nothing in this Article prevents the relevant authority from establishing, using any power the relevant authority has,[^c-456b2a8336ab80a13a7ade23ad501baf] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] additional conditions notably with regard to production method, in particular with a view to taking into account the need to meet the objectives of Directive 91/676/EEC and Directive 2000/60/EC, given the potential of nitrogen-fixing crops to increase the risk of nitrogen leaching in the autumn. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Areas with nitrogen-fixing crops shall not include the areas covered with equivalent practices mentioned in points I.3 and 4 of Annex IX to Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(10a.) For the purposes of paragraphs 2, 5 and 7, ‘no agricultural production’ means no agricultural activity as defined in Article 4(1)(c)(i) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, without prejudice to the requirements defined under GAEC 4 as referred to in Annex II to Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. Actions aiming at establishing a green soil cover for biodiversity purposes, including sowing mixtures of wild flower seeds, shall be allowed. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Nothing in this Article prevents the relevant authority, using any power the relevant authority has, from allowing[^c-0b1ab479430b75304db3c807a6ec26c2] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] cutting or grazing on buffer strips and field margins as well as on strips of eligible hectares along forest edges without production, provided that the strip remains distinguishable from adjacent agricultural land. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(10b.) The use of plant protection products shall be prohibited on all areas referred to in paragraphs 2, 9 and 10 as well as on areas with agricultural production referred to in paragraph 7. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(10c.) On areas referred to in paragraph 9 established by under-sowing grass or leguminous crops in the main crop, this prohibition shall apply from the moment of the harvesting of the main crop for at least 8 weeks or until the sowing of the next main crop. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(11.) A farmer can declare the same area or landscape feature only once in one claim year for the purpose of complying with the ecological focus area requirement. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

12.[^c-690041dad4cf947629921fdc768b96e7] The relevant authority shall specify the species that are to be used for afforested areas. Afforested areas shall be arable land eligible for the basic payment scheme referred to in Chapter 1 of Title III of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and fulfilling the conditions under the small or farm woodland creation option of the forestry grant scheme applied to the species specified by the relevant authority. The forestry grant scheme agreement referred to in Article 46(2)(h) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 must have been issued after 1 January 2015. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-b6af5a48a93de15405e4918e2bafff5c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
