## CHAPTER 4 (affected by repeal, see note) — PAYMENT FOR YOUNG FARMERS (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

### Article 49 (affected by repeal, see note). Access of legal persons to the payment for young farmers (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) The annual payment for young farmers referred to in Article 50(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 shall be granted to a legal person irrespective of its legal form if the following conditions are fulfilled: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- ((a)) the legal person is entitled to a payment under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] basic payment scheme *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-400640f5d80ad85f9d4df739a0c6c10b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] farm sustainability transition payment scheme *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-d2e38caba56829834ece51ad01041af1]... referred to in Chapter 1 of Title III of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and has activated payment entitlements or declared eligible hectares, as referred to in Article 50(4) of that Regulation; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((b)) a young farmer within the meaning of Article 50(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 exercises effective and long-term control over the legal person in terms of decisions related to management, benefits and financial risks in each year in respect of which the legal person applies for the payment under the young farmers scheme. Where several natural persons, including person(s) who are not young farmer(s), participate in the capital or management of the legal person, the young farmer(s) shall in each year in respect of which the legal person applies for the payment under the young farmers scheme be capable of exercising such effective and long-term control either solely or jointly together with other farmers[^c000006] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-248437395cda20ad21c7e583811df17c]...;
- ((c)) at least one of the young farmers fulfilling the condition set out in point (b) meets the eligibility criteria established by *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-0897e1e05b8ec668cfc302c3f952e654] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] pursuant to Article 50(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, if any, unless *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority has[^c-2faf7ea16dd8f2f7f2d58ef9e919caaa] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] decided that those criteria shall apply to all such young farmers. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where a legal person is solely or jointly controlled by another legal person, the conditions set out in point (b) of the first subparagraph shall apply to any natural person having control over that other legal person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1a.)[^c-2233f1b1ec952c40fd7450b0b2862fcc] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(2.) The payment referred to in Article 50(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 shall no longer be granted if all young farmers complying with the criteria set out in point (b) of the first subparagraph of paragraph 1 and, where appropriate, in point (c) of the first subparagraph of paragraph 1 have ceased control over the legal person. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3.) For the purposes of this Article: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- ((a)) any reference in Article 50(4) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] , (5), (6)[^c-eaeb303e3d50053c2a12895087aa1bca] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15], (8)[^c-a147b6d225d65697f229414917181880] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] and (9) *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 to ‘farmer’ shall be construed as a reference to the legal person referred to in this Article; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((b)) the reference to the first submission of an application to the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] basic payment scheme *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-331a7599fd4ba40cf589944aa4569a76] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] farm sustainability transition payment scheme *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-fcb91591638562110dd526082c60e1f4]... referred to in Article 50(2)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 shall be construed as a reference to the legal person’s first application for the payment under the young farmers scheme; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((c)) without prejudice to paragraph 4 of this Article, the reference in the second sentence of Article 50(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 to ‘setting up’ shall be construed as a reference to the setting up by the young farmers having control over the legal person in accordance with point (b) of the first subparagraph of paragraph 1 of this Article. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

A young farmer who exercises effective and long-term control over the legal person within the meaning of point (b) of the first subparagraph of paragraph 1 of this Article shall, for the purposes of Article 50(2)(b) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, be no more than 40 years of age in the year of the first submission of an application under the *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] basic payment scheme *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-331a7599fd4ba40cf589944aa4569a76] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] farm sustainability transition payment scheme *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] or the single area payment scheme by that legal person with a young farmer in control. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(4.) Where several young farmers as referred to in point (b) of the first subparagraph of paragraph 1 have acquired control over the legal person at different moments of time, the earliest acquisition of control shall be considered as the time of ‘setting up’ referred to in the second sentence of Article 50(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

### Article 50 (affected by repeal, see note). Access of a group of natural persons to the payment for young farmers (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Article 49 shall apply mutatis mutandis in respect of a group of natural persons as referred to in Article 4(1)(a) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 for which the requirements laid down in Article 49(1)(a) of this Regulation are met at the level of the group. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

## CHAPTER 5 (affected by repeal, see note) — COUPLED SUPPORT (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

### SECTION 1 (affected by repeal, see note) — Voluntary coupled support (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 51 (affected by repeal, see note). Definitions (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For the purposes of this Section, ‘coupled support measures’ means measures implementing the voluntary coupled support referred to in Article 52(1) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 52 (affected by repeal, see note). General principles (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) The regions referred to in Article 52(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] are the regions defined by the relevant authority[^c-711649716d4f3c4c57bc44ffb1973e5f] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] prior to exit day *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-22deab8eb3e0d4a248776e18aed04ef9] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15], in accordance with objective and non-discriminatory criteria such as the agronomic and socioeconomic characteristics and the regional agricultural potential, or the institutional or administrative structure. Such regions may differ from regions established under other support schemes provided for in Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) When defining the specific types of farming or specific agricultural sectors referred to in Article 52(3) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-825dee36bc36343c1524585c3a5da94c] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall take into account in particular the relevant production structures and conditions of the region or sector concerned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3.)[^c000008] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

#### Article 53 (affected by repeal, see note). Conditions for granting the support (affected by repeal, see note)[^M_E_388db846-5bb0-4bea-cb10-4a0292bab66c][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

*Extent: E+W+S*

(1.) The eligibility criteria for coupled support measures means the criteria defined by the relevant authority[^c-25fd4e15ce225d43c425851c8b4e2e30] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] with the framework set out in Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and the conditions laid down in this Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(2.) Areas and yields and number of animals referred to in Article 52(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 shall be *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] jointly agreed by the relevant authorities for each constituent nation[^c-0b3a37aa7f5621af5a5c965a39005aec] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] at regional or sector level. They shall reflect the maximum yields, area cultivated or number of animals reached in the targeted region or sector in at least one year in the period of five years preceding the year of the decision referred to in Article 53(1) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The annual payment shall be expressed as the per unit amount of support. It may be either one of the following amounts, or, when the area or the number of animals eligible for the support does not exceed the area or the number of animals fixed as referred to in the first subparagraph of this paragraph, an amount between them: *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- ((a)) the ratio between the amount fixed for the financing of the measure as notified according to point (3)(i) of Annex I to this Regulation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] as it had effect immediately before exit day[^c-8228d378908169511b63b7e2918083db] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] and the area or the number of animals eligible for the support in the year in question; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- ((b)) the ratio between the amount fixed for the financing of the measure as notified according to point (3)(i) of Annex I to this Regulation *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] as it had effect immediately before exit day[^c-bd3f4bbdb4e414d002c164614875bb96] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] and the area or the number of animals fixed as referred to in the first subparagraph of this paragraph. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Without prejudice to Article 52(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, for the per unit amount of support referred to in the second subparagraph of this paragraph, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority may decide, prior to exit day,[^c-ef74d6ddca23ea43ca95bec3cb3de2c6] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] to apply modulated per unit amounts in respect of certain categories of farmers or at farm level in order to take into account economies of scale resulting from the size of the production structures in the targeted specific type of farming or specific agricultural sector, or, if the measure targets a region or an entire sector, in the region or sector concerned. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-82bdb21b2fe9f7960babde56ac939f9e]...

(3.)[^c-3d1ca5d677b6b7f128e09bae2dbc82fa] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(4.) Where the coupled support measure concerns bovine animals and/or sheep and goats, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-4121f20bce65c20bfb77434869a8d793]... the requirements to identify and register animals provided for in Regulation (EC) No 1760/2000 of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^f00028] or Council Regulation (EC) No 21/2004 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^f00029] respectively *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall apply[^c-c1640ee44eb8a56fa5a3f614f657f0bf] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15].

[^c-c836e153305a283d6a240a4dff3a103d]...

By 15 September 2015, Member States shall notify the Commission of the dates referred to in the second subparagraph.[^c000009] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(5.) The relevant authority[^c-b2aa5523f0fbb4e9f8418d6a57157f98] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may not grant area-related coupled support for areas that are not eligible areas within the meaning of Article 32(2), (3) and (4) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-c60a88b06542a8d848e3e5f71ac79463]...

#### Article 53. Conditions for granting the support[^M_E_37894638-8430-4685-bcda-0b2962e4e59c]

*Extent: N.I.*

(1.) The eligibility criteria for coupled support measures means the criteria defined by the relevant authority[^V_NI_466ac25b-58c9-3174-ee01-fc7970b0fbe8] with the framework set out in Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and the conditions laid down in this Regulation.

(2.)[^V_NI_1d4ff097-3b67-8f1b-9fdf-352cd9838b8e] Areas and yields and number of animals referred to in Article 52(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 shall be fixed by the relevant authority at a regional or sector level.

(3.)[^V_NI_9c3e338a-5b2c-d7e6-8b59-ffe02d42e136] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

Where the coupled support measure concerns bovine animals and/or sheep and goats, [^V_NI_970191bb-dec1-988d-36d3-f569c2289e8e]... the requirements to identify and register animals provided for in Regulation (EC) No 1760/2000 of the European Parliament and of the Council[^f00028] or Council Regulation (EC) No 21/2004[^f00029] respectively shall apply[^V_NI_a52eadb9-5696-9d5a-88e4-d0fbadcfd891].

[^V_NI_a8b83d46-9951-b580-a097-82076daa405b]...

By 15 September 2015, Member States shall notify the Commission of the dates referred to in the second subparagraph.[^V_NI_7f99ec4c-3445-842e-6471-3a39adc76986]

(5.) The relevant authority[^V_NI_67e0da42-7368-5392-0428-ae79e3878085] may not grant area-related coupled support for areas that are not eligible areas within the meaning of Article 32(2), (3) and (4) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. [^V_NI_7ac84de4-3ee9-b176-4cd2-80d83da08b4c]...

(1.) Without prejudice to the requirements set out in Chapter I of Title IV of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority may decide,[^c-4df31efdbb678f5b9e2f0a9ae1381fec] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] if jointly agreed by the relevant authorities for each constituent nation *(Extent: NI)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-ee4a794008c2de5120ae67b534bb7c51] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15], to use the amounts fixed in accordance with Article 53 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] for the purpose of financing one or several other support measures under Chapter I of Title IV of *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] that Regulation[^c-db58cdb8a6887671935fb948471e08e5] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] in respect of the same claim year. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

#### Article 54 (affected by repeal, see note). Consistency and cumulation of support (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(1.) For the purposes of Article 52(8) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, measures provided for in Regulation (EU) No 1305/2013 and Regulation (EU) No 1308/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^f00030] shall be considered as ‘other *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-9f19a998cb42eaff1adef2d59c3a1cc7]... measures and policies’. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- ((b)) different coupled support measures; *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The relevant authority *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall ensure that coupled support measures do not interfere with the proper functioning of other measures mentioned in the first subparagraph. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(3.) Where support under a certain coupled support measure may also be granted under another coupled support measure, or under a measure implemented under other *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] [^c-7d526c99a6a9fbe8cc13d961c6af125d]... measures and policies, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] the relevant authority[^c-27439a71c1159b44e01a3e5204f425de] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] shall ensure that the farmer concerned may receive support aiming at the objective referred to in Article 52(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 under only one such measure per sector, region, specific type of farming or specific agricultural sector that is targeted in accordance with Article 52(3) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

## CHAPTER 6 (affected by repeal, see note) — NOTIFICATIONS (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

### Article 62 (affected by repeal, see note). Notifications relating to definitions and related provisions (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Member States shall notify the Commission of any decisions taken in accordance with Article 4(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 by 31 January 2015. Such notification shall include the details of such decisions, their justification and the objective criteria on the basis of which those decisions have been made. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

### Article 63 (affected by repeal, see note). Notifications relating to the reduction coefficient according to Article 32(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Member States shall notify the Commission of any decisions pursuant to Article 8 by 31 January 2015. Such a notification shall include the details of such decisions, their justification and the objective criteria on the basis of which those decisions have been made. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Where a Member State decides to grant the payment for areas with natural constraints pursuant to Chapter 4 of Title III of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 it shall notify the Commission by 1 August 2014 of any decision taken in accordance with Article 48 of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013. Such a notification shall comprise the details of such decision including, where appropriate, information on any restriction of the payments to certain areas pursuant to Article 48(2) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, on any application of the maximum limits referred to in Article 48(4) of that Regulation and on any regional application pursuant to Article 48(5) of that Regulation. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

## ANNEX III (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note) — ... method for the quantitative determination of the Δ9-tetrahydrocannabinol content in hemp varieties (affected by repeal, see note) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^c-278a66bf3cef1b6d194d5dbd6365f606][^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b][^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

1\.

**Scope *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]**

The method set out in this Annex seeks to determine the Δ9-tetrahydrocannabinol (hereinafter referred to as THC) content of varieties of hemp ( *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] *Cannabis sativa *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]* L.). As appropriate, the method involves applying procedure A or B as described in this Annex. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The method is based on the quantitative determination of THC by gas chromatography (GC) after extraction with a suitable solvent. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

1.1.

*Procedure A *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]*

Procedure A shall be used for checks on the production of hemp as referred to in Article 32(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and Article 30(g) of Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) No 809/2014 *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15][^f00032].

1.2.

*Procedure B *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]*

Procedure B shall be used in cases as referred to in Article 36(6) of Implementing Regulation (EU) No 809/2014. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

2\.

**Sampling *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]**

2.1.

*Samples *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]*

The samples shall be taken during the day following a systematic pattern to ensure that the sample is representative of the field, but excluding the edges of the crop. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

2.1.1.

Procedure A: in a standing crop of a given variety of hemp, a 30 cm part containing at least one female inflorescence of each plant selected shall be taken. Sampling shall be carried out during the period running from 20 days after the start of flowering to 10 days after the end of flowering. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The relevant authority[^M_F_2f755c7c-2f3d-4281-8f7b-c232996bb993] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may authorise sampling to be carried out during the period from the start of flowering to 20 days after the start of flowering provided that, for each variety grown, other representative samples are taken in accordance with the first subparagraph during the period from 20 days after the start of flowering to 10 days after the end of flowering. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

For hemp cultivated as catch crop, in the absence of female inflorescences, the top 30 cm of the plant stem shall be taken. In that case sampling shall be carried out just before the end of the vegetation period, once the leaves begin presenting the first signs of yellowing, however no later than the onset of a forecast period of frost. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

2.1.2.

Procedure B: in a standing crop of a given variety of hemp, the upper third of each plant selected shall be taken. Sampling shall be carried out during the 10 days following the end of flowering or, for hemp cultivated as catch crop, in the absence of female inflorescences, just before the end of the vegetation period, once the leaves begin presenting the first sign of yellowing, but no later than the onset of a forecast period of frost. In the case of dioecious varieties, only female plants shall be taken. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

2.2.

*Sample size *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]*

Procedure A: the sample shall comprise parts of 50 plants per field. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Procedure B: the sample shall comprise parts of 200 plants per field. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Each sample shall be placed in a fabric or paper bag, without crushing it, and be sent to the laboratory for analysis. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] relevant authority[^M_F_faf9ba3e-be6b-4340-9a15-4181bb096bf7] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15] may provide for a second sample to be collected for counteranalysis, if required, to be kept either by the producer or by the body responsible for the analysis. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

2.3.

*Drying and storage of the sample *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]*

Drying of the samples shall begin as soon as possible and, in any case, within 48 hours using any method below 70 °C. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Samples shall be dried to a constant weight and to a moisture content of between 8 % and 13 %. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

After drying, the samples shall be stored without crushing them at below 25 °C in a dark place. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

3\.

**Determination of THC content *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]**

3.1.

*Preparation of the test sample *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]*

Stems and seeds over 2 mm in size shall be removed from the dried samples. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The dried samples shall be grinded to obtain a semi-fine powder (passing through a 1 mm mesh sieve). *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The powder may be stored for 10 weeks at below 25 °C in a dark, dry place. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

3.2.

*Reagents and extraction solution *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]*

Reagents *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- Δ9-tetrahydrocannabinol, pure for chromatographic purposes, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- squalane, pure for chromatographic purposes, as an internal standard. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

Extraction solution *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- 35 mg of squalane per 100 ml hexane. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

3.3.

*Extraction of THC *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]*

100 mg of the powdered test sample shall be weighed, be placed in a centrifuge tube and 5 ml of extraction solution shall be added containing the internal standard. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The sample shall be placed in an ultrasound bath and be left for 20 minutes. It shall be centrifuged for 5 minutes at 3 000 r.p.m. and then the supernatant THC solution shall be removed. The solution shall be injected into the chromatograph and a quantitative analysis shall be carried out. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

3.4.

*Gas chromatography *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]*

(a)

Apparatus *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- gas chromatograph with a flame ionisation detector and a split/splitless injector, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- column allowing good separation of cannabinoids, for example a glass capillary column 25 m long and 0,22 mm in diameter impregnated with a 5 % non-polar phenyl-methyl-siloxane phase. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(b)

Calibration ranges *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

At least three points for procedure A and five points for procedure B, including points 0,04 and 0,50 mg/ml THC in extraction solution. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(c)

Experimental conditions *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

The following conditions are given as an example for the column referred to in (a): *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- oven temperature 260 °C, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- injector temperature 300 °C, *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
- detector temperature 300 °C. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

(d)

Volume injected: 1 μl. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

4\.

**Results *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]**

The findings shall be expressed to two decimal places in grams of THC per 100 grams of analytical sample dried to constant weight. A tolerance of 0,03 g per 100 g shall apply. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- Procedure A: one determination per test sample. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

However, where the result obtained is above the limit laid down in Article 32(6) of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013, a second determination shall be carried out per analysis sample and the mean value of the two determinations shall be taken as the result. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]

- Procedure B: the result shall correspond to the mean value of two determinations per test sample. *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-59cb7aeaef627642513536c83c61e31b] *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-30f4b2f5830f12509d522ae770688d15]
